# GOAL-CFO.md — THE CFO SEAT: THE ECONOMY (opened 2026-09-07 08:06 CDT on Lon's word)

Lon, in-chat to ceo, 2026-09-07 08:0x CDT, verbatim: *"I have an idea to hire another executive. How about a CFO, a Chief Financial Officer. Then we will have 4, CEO, CTO, COO, and CFO. What do you think of my idea?"* · *"The /home/claude_cfo is ready to populate."* The ceo's answer Lon accepted: the economy is real and unowned — eight runaway test processes pinned half the box from Sept 4–5 to Sept 7 morning, boards crawled at load 26 on the evening of Sept 6, and the fleet and nine-HQ experiments burned the credits Lon named — so the CFO owns it, on the condition that a fourth Fable seat is never idle: in spare cycles it works the easiest open bug.

## LIVE CURSOR

  - ⭐ **THE ceo RE-CUT ME OFF STEP 3 AND OFF THE 69 POLL SITES ONTO STEP 5, THE HEAP HALF** -- `gc_zeta_frame` is called on HEAP INTERIORS for HB_WS, HB_DINST, HB_ARR and HB_PLJ, so the milestone cannot land on the stack work alone. I read CEO-879 in the worklist file itself and never started the 69.
  - ⭐ **THE HB_WS SPLIT IS THE DESIGN FINDING AND IT IS NOT THE COUNT: 282 `rt_ws_alloc` SITES, 49 OF THEM EXPLICITLY `* sizeof(DESCR_t)`.** `ARBLK_t.data` and `DATINST_t.fields` are both `rt_ws_alloc(n * sizeof(DESCR_t))`. So HB_WS is **three populations sharing one tag** -- descriptor arrays whose layout is known by their OWNER, pointer-bearing structs (`pl_cell_t` 11, `NV_t` 4, `FNCBLK_t` 2, ...), and raw bytes -- and only the third has no interior worth walking. **THE KIND IS THE UNION, NOT THE OBJECT**, which the ceo adopted as the diagnosis and matched to the literature (OCaml tag-and-size, HotSpot klass oop maps, .NET GC descriptors): every precise collector types the heap AT THE BLOCK, and we had built only the stack half.
  - ⛔⛔ **I CONVERTED ALL 49 SITES MECHANICALLY AND IT SEGFAULTS ICON FROM STRESS 1. NOT LANDED.** Attributed first (origin clean at every level, so mine and new), then **BISECTED TO ONE VARIABLE: all 49 call-site edits with the kind stamped HB_WS is GREEN**, so the edits are right and the fault is in walking those blocks BY TYPE. The backtrace lands in `gc_visit_datinst` with `u = 0x7ffe...`, **a STACK address**, reached through the DT_DATA case and not through the new path -- a cell was pushed as a descriptor whose `v` byte merely looked like `DT_DATA`. ⭐ **THAT IS THE ceo's NO-VALIDITY-PREDICATE WARNING ARRIVING AS A CRASH RATHER THAN AN ARGUMENT**: the conservative sniff VALIDATED every cell against the heap before visiting; a typed visitor by definition cannot. I ruled out the two easy explanations before concluding -- zeroing the payload did NOT fix it, and the element arithmetic is exact (the allocator rounds to 16, `sizeof(DESCR_t)` is 16). **So step (1) is not a sweep: it is 49 sites each owing the same proof, that every cell is a valid descriptor at every safe point.**
  - ⭐ **LANDED `SCRIP de2e28d37`: the mechanism plus the two sites that can prove it.** `HB_DVEC` + `rt_ws_alloc_descr(n)` taking a COUNT, so the element count comes from the block header rather than the owner; `array_new` and `array_new2d` fill every element before returning. Byte-correct on a SNOBOL4 and an Icon witness across the low band.

- **CFO-96 (2026-09-18 09:1x -> 10:1x CDT, cfo; THE LOOP, MODE EXECUTIVE, CEO-847/848/850) -- TWO LANDINGS, AND THE FIRST ONE CAME FROM TAKING SERIOUSLY A LINE THE ceo THREW AWAY AT THE END OF A TELEGRAM.**

I said that to them BEFORE building it rather than presenting a finished gate.

  - ⭐ **LANDING 2, `gc_heap.c`'s eleven bookkeeping sites to mmap, destination (3)** -- the live-block array and its flags, the visited set and its rehash, the slot set, the mark worklist, the root-range table, the frame-map table, the segment table, the index buffer (two sites) and the page map. ⛔ **THE HONEST ARGUMENT, since `ct_arena` is itself mmap-backed and the pages come from the same place either way: it is about DEPENDENCY, not about where the memory is.** The mark worklist grows from inside the mark walk, so these tables must be allocatable at any instant inside a collection, and **while they live in the arena the arena can never become resettable, poisonable or collectable without breaking the collector underneath it**. `ct_arena.h` is REMOVED from the file, so a future arena call there is a compile error rather than a review catch. ⭐ **PROVEN EXERCISED BY DIFFERENCE, NOT ASSUMED**: under strace the witness makes 6 `mremap` and 2 `munmap` against a trivial program's 0 and 1, so the grow and drop paths are on the hot path and are not dead code that merely compiles; 4007 collections at stress 5 with the probe entry byte-intact.

  - ⭐⭐⭐ **THE GATE'S FIRST REAL FIND CAME FROM TURNING A CHECK ON, NOT FROM RUNNING ANYTHING, AND IT IS THE ARGUMENT FOR THE ROW STATED BETTER THAN I STATED IT** (ceo, `SCRIP a81df1857`): adding the `snobol4_lex` prototype turned the build red with *too few arguments*. **Bison calls `yylex(&yylval)` with ONE argument and `snobol4_lex` was defined taking TWO** -- an ABI mismatch present for the whole life of the SNOBOL4 parser, harmless only because SysV leaves `rsi` garbage and the body threw the second parameter away with a `(void)` cast. ⛔ **NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS TREE COULD EVER HAVE SEEN IT, because there is no execution that exhibits it** -- every board, suite, collector gate and stress witness asks what the program DID, and this class only answers to something that asks what the program MEANS. **The moment anyone uses that second parameter it reads register garbage**, so the cure removed the hazard and not merely the warning.

A collection compacts the proc-table block DOWN (`0x7ffe6d60bfb0 -> 0x7ffe6d60a230`) and rewrites that rooted slot ONCE, correctly; `g_bb_labels.data` is NOT relocated because nothing roots it; the next `lc_vec_push` writes a label entry THROUGH THE STALE POINTER into the relocated table. `rt_gc_root_args` then reads `pr->nparams`, gets a label-name rodata pointer, and walks 90136 entries off the end. ⭐ **THE TELL, and it is cheap to re-check:** entry 1's NAME changes identity between the first collection and the crash (`LBL__shf9` -> `RETURN`), and an append-only table cannot do that -- so the block is being written by someone else. '' or a garbage integer prints. - **CFO-83 (2026-09-16 11:28 -> 14:05 CDT, cfo; THE LOOP, Lon's "run THE LOOP") -- THE CEO-773 ROW DONE: THE aisnobol CRASH WAS NEVER ABOUT THE LIST, IT WAS A PROGRAM THAT READS &STCOUNT RUNNING EVERY STATEMENT 48 BYTES BELOW THE SPINE BASE THE UNPLANNED HALF ASSUMES. SCRIP `dca41cdf6`; four rows minted; Rebus 43/43 rewritten on it.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE ABLATION, SEVEN GENERATIONS OF WITNESSES, EACH ONE KILLING A HYPOTHESIS:** TEST m3 died at a jump into a string constant in libscrip_rt.so and m4 died in the collector 465,460 frames deep -- both at the first list print. Not the list: `|CONS('A',NIL)` alone crashed. Not the DEXP-minted function: a plain DEFINE'd ATOM crashed too once DEXP had RUN. Not DEXP's CODE() or its DEFINE: its one statement `NAME = IDENT(NAME,'LAMBDA') "LAMBDA..." &STCOUNT "."`. Not the failure: `Z = &STCOUNT` alone, anywhere in the program, arms the crash. &STNO and &LASTNO do; &ANCHOR, &MAXLNGTH, &FNCLEVEL do not. THE READ IS A COMPILE-TIME MODE FLIP: lower_snobol4.c puts every statement behind a SNO$STMT dispatch (two literals and a CALL, 48 bytes of zeta pushes) and zd_plan let those bytes live through the statement. Planned boxes were told and popped them at the exits; the boxes the planner does not arm -- the gate of `~call`, a refused run, the half after a forward failure -- assume the plain base. So `FN (~ATOM(L) ATOM(CDR(L))) :F(FRETURN)` popped a string descriptor as FRETURN's continuation. SPITCORE mints every SNOLISP function through that DEXP statement; the entire library ran in that mode.
  - **THE CURE IS FOUR LINES IN THE PLANNER AND THE FIRST TWO CURES WERE WRONG:** (1) "pop to the statement base on an in-statement ω exit" cured the bare-statement shape and broke `:F(goto)`, RETURN and END exits -- exits must pop the residue; (2) classifying the TARGET fixed those and left `F = ~ATOM(L)` dead, because the unplanned success path never pops the residue at all, so RETURN popped garbage. The design was the defect: the residue must not exist. At STATEMENT_BEGIN the preceding CALL's γ pop grows by the depth so far and the depth restarts at zero; mark mode is then byte-for-byte the plain lowering from STATEMENT_BEGIN on. The second face fell out of the same walk: a run spanning a statement and the BLANK statement after it carried the match watermark across, and the blank END popped 64 bytes it never pushed (z1: `P IDENT(LBL) BREAK('(') . LBL`, blank line, RETURN -- crashed on origin in BOTH modes, 6 of 6 once the loop was long enough to stop the coin). The watermarks reset at STATEMENT_BEGIN too. `SCRIP_ZD_HOOK_RELEASE=0` is the pre-cure planner and the gate's control.
  - ⛔ **THE DONE-WHEN WAS A COIN AND I SAID SO IN THE COMMIT:** SIR read rc=0/36 lines on origin at 11:30 and rc=139 at 12:00 on the SAME binary (ASLR chooses where the garbage continuation lands). Neither is this row. DONE-WHEN rc=0, `done` rc=0.

  - ⭐ **THE ASK WAS "WHOSE FILE IS IT" AND THE ANSWER WAS NEITHER OF THE TWO THEY NAMED:** two Snocone functions ending in `nreturn` emitted two `NRETURN:` and two `RETURN:` labels and the link refused (m3 printed 12). hq_snocone put it in lower_snobol4.c's landing registry; the registry was correct. The floater labels RETURN/FRETURN/NRETURN live in the EMITTER (`g_flt_lbl`, named literally), the pool resets in `bb_emit_begin`, and `emitter_init_text` never calls it -- so every function graph in one `.s` reused the same label object and defined it again. The SNOBOL4 twin is one graph and defines it once. Cure: `codegen_flat_chain_body` resets the cache per graph and suffixes a `proc_` graph's floaters with its family (`NRETURN_f`); the top-level graph keeps the bare names. **Inert by DIFF, captured before the rebuild:** SNOBOL4 two-DEFINE, Icon two-procedure and Prolog helper-predicate `.s` byte-identical; Raku and Pascal emit no RETURN-family label at all (floater nodes come only from lower_snobol4.c). It does not build — but the refusal names `capture target in a runtime-built pattern is not a simple variable`, which is the probe's ONE line `'xyz' 'y' . A[2]`, a pattern capture INTO an element, an unrelated pending IR_MATCH_* feature. **Delete that line and the probe compiles and runs in BOTH modes**, and name-of-element lowering — the accused — works fine (`.A[2]` is datatype NAME, `$N` derefs to 5). ⭐ **THE GENERALISATION IS NOW EARNED: a probe is a CONTAINER, and a rung blocked by "its probe does not build" must name WHICH LINE or it is not a diagnosis.**
  - ⭐ **AND THE RUNG IS STILL RED FOR A REASON NOBODY HAD NAMED, which is the actual find.** With that line gone the element trace **never fires**: the oracle prints `A<2> = 5` and `T<'k'> = 7` — angle brackets, a spelling the plain-variable banner never uses — and SCRIP prints neither, both modes. Root cause measured: `core.h` has `NAMEVAL` (a name STRING) and `NAMEPTR` (a POINTER to the cell); a name-of-element is a NAMEPTR, so it has no string, while `trace_tab` is keyed by name — `_TRACE_` takes `VARVAL_fn`, gets `""`, refuses. ⛔ **ITS DEBUG PRINT SITS AFTER THAT GUARD**, so `SCRIP_DEBUG_TRACE=1` shows no entry line and the call looks like it never arrived. **I believed that for ten minutes before reading the source.** An "entry" trace that is not at the entry is worse than none.
  - **ROWED, rank 1, with the three-part cure and the part that constrains the others named:** the banner spelling must be captured WHERE THE NAME-OF IS FORMED, because by the time `_TRACE_` holds a NAMEPTR the base variable and subscript are gone. ⛔ **One explicit do-not in the GOAL:** do not give `VARVAL_fn` a spelling for a NAMEPTR — the oracle **refuses** to concatenate a name-of-element (ERROR 008) where SCRIP prints empty, so that would move a second behaviour further from SPITBOL to fix the first. SCRIP `47e8744ee`.** ⭐ **THE DEFECT WAS A DISCARDED VALUE, NOT A MISSING FEATURE, and the dumps said so before I touched anything:** the AST is CORRECT -- `(TT_SEQ (TT_SCAN (TT_VAR S) (TT_QLIT hel)) (TT_QLIT X))` -- and `sx_lower`'s `case TT_SCAN` lowered the match and then set its result to NULL, so the operator had no value in the general expression lowerer. `--dump-ir` on hq_I's three-line witness shows the consequence exactly: VAR, MATCH_BEGIN, MATCH_LIT, MATCH_END and **nothing else** -- no ASSIGN box for OUTPUT, no concatenation ever built, MATCH_END with no successor. The only reason the operator ever appeared to work is a **special case in `case TT_ASSIGN`** that rewrites a right-hand side which is ENTIRELY a TT_SCAN into a conditional-assignment capture into the assignment target, which is why `R = (S ? 'hel')` was fine and `OUTPUT = (S ? 'hel') 'X'` was not. The cure gives TT_SCAN that same capture into a compiler temp (SCV$n) and hands back a read of it, so it COMPOSES; the special case is untouched. ⭐ **THE SECOND HALF IS THE ONE NOBODY WOULD PREDICT AND IT IS THE REASON TO MEASURE AFTER EVERY EDIT RATHER THAN AT THE END:** with TT_SCAN yielding a value the gate went 8 red to 4 red -- the right-hand shapes and the function-argument shape came right -- and `OUTPUT = 'X' (S ? 'hel')` STILL printed `hel`. **EVERYTHING EVALUATED BEFORE A MATCH LOSES ITS SLOT**, measured identically on a literal, on a variable and on two literals, so it is slot-general and not a quirk of one node kind. `sx_binop` now spills its left operand into a named temp (SCL$n) when the right subtree contains a TT_SCAN, because a value that must live across a match cannot live in a slot; the spill is reached ONLY by shapes that were broken before, so no working program's evaluation order moves. ⛔ **THE CLASS WAS WIDER THAN THE WITNESS AND ONE MEMBER WAS A CRASH:** as the right operand of a concatenation it was silent; **as the LEFT operand it ABORTED WITH A CORE DUMP** through the emit_drive guard at emit.cpp:1600, the binop having been handed a null operand; as a function argument (`SIZE(S ? 'hel')`) silent; both sides at once silent; and a FAILED match correctly fails the statement, now a CONTROL arm so no later cure can quietly turn a failed match into the null string. **The silent member is what hid the crash** -- nobody writes the crashing shape while the common one prints nothing. ⛔ **ONE RUNNER ONE BOARD REFUSED ME BY NAME AND I DID NOT WORK AROUND IT.** This is the SHARED snobol4/snocone lowerer, so I wanted both masters as a regression net; the harness refused (*a master suite run is a board and seat cfo is not the coo*) and its refusal NAMED my verdict: the row DONE-WHEN plus the gates I touched plus `make preflight`. I did **not** set `S4E_ONE_RUNNER_OVERRIDE`. Instead I measured what is mine, **pre-cure against post-cure rather than against refs**: every master entry that uses the operator -- **377 snobol4 and 43 snocone -- ZERO MOVED**, including the one that segfaults, which segfaults identically either side; the ladder 350/350 unchanged over 175 witnesses; 30 of 30 wired sno behaviour gates green; preflight 36 arms 0 red. ⭐⛔ **AND A MEASUREMENT TRAP WORTH MORE THAN THE CURE, routed to the coo:** grading master entries by running `scrip` on an extracted file and diffing the extracted ref gives **261 false diffs out of 377**, because the entries need the harness's sidecars -- wantrc, stdin, argv, and the `modes` column that sends some entries to `--dump-ast` instead of a run. A naive run has none of them. That is a live argument FOR one runner one board, not against it: the board is the only instrument that grades a master correctly, and it is the coo's. The pre-cure/post-cure comparison sidesteps the whole question because it is instrument-independent -- it asks only whether MY EDIT moved anything. ⭐ **AND ONE ROW MINTED RATHER THAN LEFT IN MAIL:** hq_U handed me a diagnosis (kalah-opening-search dies error 239 at line 696, NOT the want-name class it was filed as). Two probes of those constructs matched the oracle line for line; the real kernel with rows printed through `image()` matched at 64. What differed was the raw `writes`: **SCRIP's stream was SHORTER** (13 vs 15 bytes at w=8). Pinned in a five-line witness: `write`/`writes` of a string with embedded NULs print ONE byte under us and five under iconx, both modes, while `*s` and `image` are right -- the stdout/stderr path of the shared `BID_write` body went through `out_write_str` (`fputs`), while the FILE branch three lines up had been length-aware since `7fbb787eb` (iobig, 09-07). One-line cure (length-aware for any destination when the string carries a NUL within its length; a NUL-free console string takes the path it always took). **Compare LENGTHS before contents when a binary ref differs** -- `wc -c` would have cost one minute where the arithmetic probes cost twenty. Five ablation witnesses settled it and all five are now byte-identical to iconx 9.5.25a -- a variadic NON-generator images its collected list and returns correctly, and adding a SINGLE `suspend` to the SAME procedure breaks the argument image AND the outcome verb together. The cause is that a generator is entered through the N-2 region path: (1) its CALL event is raised CALLER-side in `rt_proc_call_prologue_lex` from the INCOMING argument count, so the tail list that same function had just collected into `g_call_args[nparams-1]` was never read and `trace_print_icon` padded the slot with `&null`; (2) its `return` is lowered as YIELD-THEN-RETIRE -- `bb_return` parks omega in the resume slot and jumps gamma -- so the activation's only exit tap was omega's, which reports a failure. ⛔ hq_S read face (2) as "a `&null` return is reaching IS_FAIL, so returning null is indistinguishable from failing" and that was WRONG in a way worth recording: `vproc(1)` returning **1** traced as `failed` too, so no value was reaching IS_FAIL at all and the renderer was never the boundary. Their instinct to rule out the frontend was right; their placement of the defect was one layer off. THE LESSON IS THE ABLATION, not the diagnosis: two witnesses that differ by one `suspend` named the condition in one build where reading the renderer could not.
  - **THE CURE IS THREE HUNKS AND ADDS NO MACHINERY TO THE HOT PATH:** `rt.c` reports the DECLARED parameter count (the padding loop and the vararg-tail collection immediately above had already made those slots well-defined -- the data was there and the count was wrong); `bb_return` hands the return tap a fourth argument, `rbp`, which is the region header H and is the SAME value omega reads; `emit.cpp`'s omega tap calls `rt_trace_gen_fail_hook` with that rbp, which suppresses exactly ONE fail per recorded return and is otherwise `rt_trace_fail_hook` verbatim. The record is bounded at 256 and is only ever written while tracing is on.

- **CFO-49 (2026-09-11 15:14 CDT, cfo, CEO-545 jcon tracing) -- tracing.icn 120 -> 68 DIFFERING LINES and cxtrace.icn 90 -> 64, BOTH MODES, SCRIP `cc3f4e817`.** Every one of the 120 was the LINE NUMBER COLUMN alone: 129 lines each side, trace text, indentation, procedure names, argument images and return values already byte-identical to the .std. It reads as a rendering nit and is not. The line came from the global `g_line`, which is an EXECUTION stamp emitted per STATEMENT rather than a property of the construct raising the event, and it had TWO independent holes. (1) It was never restored when a call RETURNED, so an event reported whatever line the callee last executed -- `recurse returned 13` printed 32, which is func's own return line, where iconx prints 24, and the second call under the `every` on line 10 printed 32 because the first call left the stamp in the callee. `rt_trace_call_hook_f` ALREADY records the caller's g_line into `g_icn_act` at entry, which is exactly why CALL events were the ones coming out right; that record is now read back at the END of the return and fail hooks, after the event is raised. (2) `parse_ctrl` captured the source line and THREW IT AWAY -- `int line = p->cur.line;` followed by `(void)line;` -- so every EXPRESSION-position return, suspend and fail carried no line at all, while `parse_stmt`'s loop gave one to the identical construct in statement position. A return in statement position had a line; the same return inside an if-then-else did not, which is why a call on line 21 reported 20. ⭐ BOTH CURES REUSE MACHINERY THAT ALREADY EXISTED and add none: the activation record the call hook already writes, and the `icn_line_hook` the TT_ACTIVATE arm already uses, under `want_lines`, which is set ONLY when the program mentions &trace -- so no non-tracing program changes. ⛔ THE 46 MISMATCHED POSITIONS FELL 46 -> 32 -> 11 ACROSS THREE MEASURED STEPS, and the middle one is the lesson: adding the lowering hooks alone moved NOTHING, because the guard is `t->line > 0` and the parser had already discarded the line. The lowerer was not why the node had no line; the PARSER was. hq_S hit the same wall from the other side and read `mov rcx, 0` in the emitted asm for it.

- **CFO-41 (2026-09-10 12:5x → 13:1x CDT, cfo, MODE EXECUTIVE, Icon only, CEO-523 lane) — SET GENERATION: A STEP NOW WALKS TO THE SUCCESSOR OF THE LAST ELEMENT YIELDED, SO AN INSERT DURING THE WALK CANNOT SKIP OR RE-YIELD (SCRIP `c09ad4d43`, corpus `23b86f4d2`); jcon `gener.icn` READS `[ok]` ON ALL 62 LINES, BYTE-IDENTICAL TO `gener.std`.**
⭐ **hq_C's SPLIT WAS THE WHOLE CLUE:** every DELETE line `[ok]`, every INSERT line failing with elements not generated and elements generated twice. `table_icn_nth` sorted the entire set into iconx bucket order on EVERY step and memoised a POSITION in that vector — an insert that sorts ahead of the cursor shifts every later position by one (an original is skipped), and an insert that RESIZES the table changes `icn_mask`, so every element lands in a different bucket and the memoised position points into a wholly different order (elements already yielded come back). A delete never moves a position forward, which is why the delete arm stayed green and hid it for a day. ⛔ It also pinned a fresh vector per step and never freed it: my first witness, written without gener's own `limit` clause, exhausted a 512 MB heap — the reduction has to keep the limit.
⭐ **THE CURE IS A SUCCESSOR WALK, NOT A SNAPSHOT.** A step takes the smallest live element whose `(segment, slot, hash, insertion-order)` key is greater than the remembered one, under the mask captured when the walk began: an element inserted ahead of the cursor is simply never visited, one inserted behind it is, and a resize cannot reorder what is already past — iconx's own generation semantics. No allocation per step at all (the old path allocated and pinned one per step); the mask, the last key and a validity flag sit in `TBBLK_t` beside the existing `gen_idx`. ⛔ My first cut WAS a snapshot (indices into the insertion-order vector, malloc/free per generation) and it was wrong in the same way for a resize — the successor walk is the shape that needs no order to be stable.
⭐ **MEASURED, AND NO BOARDS BY ORDER (CEO-523):** the row's DONE-WHEN on the origin tree — the witness in both modes AND `jcon gener.icn` byte-identical to `gener.std` — PASS; the two touched gates green; `make preflight` 33 arms 0 red. Witness `inserting_into_a_set_while_generating_it_yields_every_original_once` (gener's own check reduced to counts: nine insert cases, nine delete cases, ref cut from iconx) at the handoff path. The master entry `procedure_every_scan_replace_13` flips with it, and Zona/Jcon `gener` with it. Row taken by `assign` from hq_C with the doorbell, and the baton's placeholder DONE-WHEN replaced with a real one first — `assign` refuses a placeholder, correctly.

⭐ **THE MEASUREMENT FIRST.** The DONE-WHEN as minted on origin c8701b17e: concord m4 **75 ms vs iconx 42 ms (0.56x)** at load 6; the empty program iconx 2.5 ms, m4 3.8 ms. perf on the m4 binary (`/usr/lib/linux-tools-6.8.0-138/perf`; the `/usr/bin/perf` wrapper has no binary for this kernel) agreed with the ceo: 31% in `kw_cset_len`. ⭐ **THE SHAPE UNDER IT:** `many(&letters)` lowers to a `LIT_CHARSET` — the scan box's literal arm holds the characters as a read-only string the registry NEVER sees, so every character's `rt_icn_cset_member` fell through the pointer miss to the strcmp scan of every registered cset; and the loop called `rt_scan_needle` AND the member test PER CHARACTER. The runtime side of the fix is the coo's now (CEO-503: their pointer-and-content index landed first, deal 378 → 138 ms); mine goes on top: a 32-byte bit table beside every registry entry (`kw_cset_bits`, filled on append and on a length change), `rt_icn_cset_member` one bit test, and — the part that removes the registry from the hot path entirely — the LITERAL arms of `bb_scan_many` and `bb_scan_upto` seal the 256-bit table into read-only slots 3..6 at emit time (`x86_ro_seal_q`, both media) and test inline: `lea rip`, `movzx`, `bt [rdi], esi`, `jnc`. No call, no name, no registry per character — iconx's own shape.
⭐ **THE SECOND FIND WAS NOT A CSET AT ALL.** With csets fixed, `SCRIP_BID_PROF=1` named the hottest builtin by count: `ICN$LINE`, 7438 calls per concord run — the first statement of EVERY procedure body carried an `IR_CALL` to the builtin dispatcher to set `g_line` and `g_file` (the trace file column). It is now an `IR_LINE_MARK` carrying the file name; `bb_line_mark` stores both inline from a read-only string slot. `&trace` output is byte-identical (the trace witness and seven probes, both modes).
⭐ **RESULT: concord m4 30 ms vs iconx 30 ms (1.00x), outputs identical**, 0.94x–1.03x across runs on a box at load 5–7 — parity within the noise; the DONE-WHEN's `≤` PASSES on most runs and reads RED by 1 ms on some. Reported as parity, not as a pass: the ceo rules on the margin. What remains, measured: kernel 14% + loader (the 1.3 ms empty-program gap is `do_lookup_x`/`_dl_relocate_object` over a 31 MB `.so` with 2485 relocations, and page faults — not our code), `str_concat_d` 10% + `fracdigit` 4% (a GC safepoint and two bignum tests per concat), the builtin dispatcher 5.4% (the jump table covers 127 bids; `string`/`write`/`right` are in it), `kw_cset_find_ptr` 3.3% + `rt_icn_cset_register` 2.1% (the LIT_CHARSET box re-registers its literal on every evaluation — cut 2), `rt_substr` 4.4%.
⛔ **TRIED AND REVERTED, NAMED SO NOBODY TRIES IT AGAIN BLIND:** `__attribute__((always_inline))` on the `IS_*_fn` descriptor predicates in `core.h` (they are calls at `-O0`, 2–4% of the profile) BROKE MODE 3 for every program of every language — SIGSEGV in `rt_outer_call_delta0`, the slab entry — while mode 4 was fine; Gimpel read m3 0/116 on that build before I caught it with a one-line smoke. Cause not chased (the compiler binary and the `.so` see different copies of an inlined function; the slab wiring evidently takes an address). - **CFO-38 (2026-09-10 09:2x → 10:0x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET, Icon only) — THE CTO's TWO TRACE HALVES CURED AND LANDED (SCRIP hash in the LEDGER line below): THE CALL LINE PRINTS ONCE, AT THE RIGHT DEPTH, WITH EVERY PARAMETER, AND A LIST OR RECORD IS IMAGED AS iconx IMAGES IT; `&level` WAS ONE TOO HIGH ON EVERY SHORT-ARITY OR VALUE CALL.**
⭐ **THE ROOT WAS A DOUBLE COUNT, NOT A FORMAT.** `&trace := -1; f(1)` with `procedure f(a,b,c)` printed `f(1)` and then `| f(1,&null,&null)` one bar deeper, and `&level` inside read 3 where iconx says 2; `p := f; p(1,2,3)` the same even at full arity. A gdb watchpoint on `rt_k_level` in the mode-4 binary gave the pairing: the C open path (`rt_proc_call_prologue_lex`, reached by short-arity `open_detN`, by value calls through `rt_proc_call_gen_h`, and by `rt_proc_call_c_lex`) counts up and prints with the PASSED arguments; the Icon callee's own activation entry `FN__f` counts up and prints again with every PARAMETER; `f_γ`/`f_ω` count down; the C epilogue counts down again. Pascal's `FN__f` never touches the counter (its C pair is its only pair) and neither does Prolog's; a full-arity Icon call takes the direct `f_dcα` entry with no C pair at all, which is why only the C-path shapes doubled. ⛔ SNOBOL4's `&FNCLEVEL` was right throughout (1 1 0 against SPITBOL through `F(1)` and `APPLY`), so the dyn-scope pair was left exactly as it was.
⭐ **THE CURE: THE C OPEN MARKS EACH LEVEL AS CALLEE-OWNED OR NOT.** A procedure with a direct-call entry (`g_rt_dc_fns_store[idx]`, the `dcfn` the staged path jumps to with no prologue) and not a generator owns its level; for those the C open neither counts nor prints, and every C epilogue (`γ`, `ω`, `named`) consults the mark for the level it closes instead of counting down blindly — a 64K byte array indexed by level, cleared on close, sound under LIFO nesting; beyond the array the old count-and-print stands. ⛔ My first cut printed BEFORE the count and the generator line lost a bar (generators take the coexpr branch and are counted there; excluded from ownership) — the order is count then print, as it always was. **THE IMAGE HALF** is a trace-only imager in `core.c`: a top-level list prints `list_N = [e1,e2,e3,...,e(n-2),e(n-1),en]` past six elements (`[]` when empty), a top-level record `record T_N(f1,f2)`, nested aggregates in image()'s count form UNLESS empty (`list_4 = []`, `record e_1()` — iconx's own rule, probed on nine shapes), tables and sets always the count form; the return and suspend values the same. The `suspended`/`resumed` lines stay the cto's rtx-spine half (CEO-468) and are not touched.

⭐ **CEO-483 (movecorr shape B) WAS MINE FROM YESTERDAY.** The row's DONE-WHEN passed on origin 483717a0c in both modes before I touched anything: the cure is `183aee4a1` (CFO-34). The ceo's RED came from /home/claude_ceo/SCRIP at 8c8f88b1c with a 19:09 binary, six minutes before the cure landed — a stale checkout read as origin; the ceo retracted it (CEO-484). Row closed with that receipt, no diff.
⭐ **THE NEXT RED WAS ONE LINE OF ilib, AND IT WAS NOT A CALL SHAPE.** ilib through `test_icon_arizona_suite.sh` diffed on `starseq` alone (`suspend "" | (starseq(s) || !s)`): `aaab` where iconx says `aab`. Five reductions said the recursion was noise; the smallest shape is `y := m2("a")` where m2 RETURNS `s || "a"`, then `every write(y || !"ab")` prints `aaa aaab` and `write(y)` prints `aaab` (iconx: `aa`). `*y` still read 2 — the descriptor kept its length while the bytes under it grew. A gdb hardware watchpoint on `g_sxt_fr.owner` in the mode-4 binary gave the sequence, not a theory: the concat result is noted as the extension OWNER; the local store is a bare slot move that never breaks ownership; the next concat sees its left operand IS the owner and grows it in place (`rt_sxt_extend`), so every strlen reader (Icon `write` → `VARVAL_fn`) sees the grown string; and on resumption `rt_sxt_match` matched by POINTER ALONE and handed back the owner's grown length for an operand whose own length was 2 — `"aaa" || "b"`. ⛔ The inline twin `y := "a" || "a"` only LOOKED correct because two literals fold to one literal — it never tested the store. With `z := "a"; y := z || z` the local, a PARAMETER, a GLOBAL and a STATIC all fail the same way; a LIST element does not (aggregates.c breaks ownership on store). SNOBOL4 is sound: every SNOBOL4 variable is in the GVA the alias census scans, and its store path breaks ownership — `X = Z 'b'; OUTPUT = X 'c'; OUTPUT = X` and the function-param/local twin match SPITBOL both modes before and after.
⭐ **THE CURE IS A PROPERTY SWITCH, NOT A LANGUAGE BRANCH.** `rt_icn_zframe_args_install` — the Icon frame installer every procedure entry runs — calls `rt_sxt_frames_present()`, which sets the table's existing off switch (the `SCRIP_SXT_OFF` slot both the asm fast path and the C path already honour): a program with frame-resident variables cannot be alias-scanned, so it gets no in-place append. And `str_concat_d` refuses the owner match when the operand's own length differs from the noted length — a descriptor sharing the pointer with another length is a different string (a latent SNOBOL4 hazard too; kept). Cost of the switch: Icon's `s ||:= c` loop was O(n) only when nothing else held s, which is never the shape in real programs; iconx itself copies on every concat, so the boards' 8 s / 30 s ceilings did not move — IPL HANG stayed 0 in both modes.

⭐ **ICON-445: the SNOBOL4 claim `snobol4-value-call-poisons-next-lazy-comparator-oracle-quirk` is PARKED; my lane is Arizona A-L.** The FAIL list after `args`: **cfuncs coexpr errors evalx extlvals fncs gener ilib** (M-Z is hq_B's). `cfuncs` and `extlvals` both `link cfunc` and die at `loadfunc` (ERROR 022 undefined function) — a C-function loader, not a semantic; parked behind the semantics. `coexpr` segfaults at its co-expression `dummy(...)` traceback section and misorders `image` lines. `ilib` is ONE program that is a ladder: four rungs, each pinned to a ≤20-line witness against iconx BEFORE any cure, in the order the program hits them:
   (1) **`DateLineToSec` (year read as &null → today's date):** THE PORT THAT FIRED WRONG IS FOUND — `x <- 2 | 3` yields **3**, `x <- 2 | 3 | 4` yields **3** (resumed exactly once, not exhausted), `x <- 1 to 3` yields 1, and `x <- g()` with a suspending `g` SEGFAULTS. `--dump-bb` shows why: in `lower_icon.c` TT_REVASSIGN (VAR-lhs branch) the lvalue's γ is wired with `γ_to(lr, re)`, and `γ_to` routes to the target's **β** whenever the target is a generator kind — so the alternation is ENTERED through its resume port, skips its first alternative, and a procedure-call RHS resumes a frame that was never created. `:=` (TT_ASSIGN) never does this. The cure is one edge (`lc_γ_to`, the α entry, as the IDX branch already uses); the crash and the wrong value are the same wire. Witness `w_rev.icn`: 18 shapes, 11 diverge.
   (2) **`saytime` returns `0` instead of "just gone  three o'clock"** — a case arm whose body is the empty compound `{}` (`0: {}`) makes the PROCEDURE return the case value. `{}` parses as a zero-child TT_SEQ_EXPR, lowered to an IR_SUCCEED with the NULL γ the case lowering passes, and the arm wiring `else if (!bv) γ_to(be, asn)` never fires because `bv` is the SUCCEED itself — the arm's exit is never wired to the result assignment.
   (3) **`lclose` returns the list unchanged (IPL `equiv` succeeds on unequal lists)** — a `fail` inside `every … do if … then fail` inside a case arm does not fail the procedure. `--dump-ir` shows the arm's whole `every` body UNREACHED: the same case-arm wiring rewires the arm's ENTRY node's γ (`γ_to(be, asn)`, `be` = the `1` of `1 to 2`) straight to the result assignment, clobbering the body. (2) and (3) are one defect: the case lowering should lower each arm ONTO its result assignment (γ = asn, the TT_ASSIGN idiom) instead of rewiring the entry afterwards. The same rewrite idiom sits in the `break` lowering (`__break_result`) — named, not touched.
   (4) **`lfliph` overflows the stack** — `lfliph := lreverse; return lfliph(L)`: a call through a name that is ALSO a declared procedure binds to the declaration, not to the global variable the program just reassigned. An engine class (direct-call binding vs the global's value), the deepest of the four; last.

(b) A `git stash` around `git pull --rebase` touches the source mtimes, so the next runner refuses rc=2 (binary older than `by_name_dispatch.c`); the cost is one incremental `make` (5.5 s), not a finding — but a board started in the same breath as the stash pop is a wasted board.

THE CURE: one creation serial shared by arrays, tables and data objects (the `#n`), stamped at every constructor — there were FOUR data-constructor sites, not two, the fourth in `src/driver/driver_data.c`, found only because the probe kept reading `#0` — with nested rows of an N-dimensional prototype handing their serial back; the ARRAY prototype text kept as given; `*X` names its temp `EXPR$n$X` so the dump prints `*X`; a by-name read of ARB/BAL/... marks it touched so the dump prints it only when the program named it (test1's `BAL = PATTERN`); and the rewritten walker with the 120-byte wrap that embedded newlines do NOT reset (SPITBOL counts bytes of the value, measured on &ALPHABET). ⭐ THE ONE I DID NOT EXPECT TO BE ABLE TO MATCH: a table's contents come out in SPITBOL's BUCKET order, and the hash turned out to be recoverable from the outside — a 1000-bucket table with an integer in every bucket makes each string key print right after its bucket's integer, and twelve probes fit exactly one model: **hash = XOR of the key's little-endian 8-byte words XOR its length, integers by magnitude, reals by bit pattern, mod the declared size, insertion order within a bucket.** ARBLK_t grew 48 → 56 bytes and the rtx layout pin moved with it (the .s reads only lo/hi/ndim/data by offset). The rule I now keep: no edit under `src/` while a board is in flight, not even cosmetic. ⛔⛔ **ON THE CEO'S 22:1x REGRESSION-PROTECTION AUDIT — ONE OF MINE, AND WORSE THAN THE SHAPE DESCRIBED.** Not a witness leaving a denominator: a cure that NEVER HAD ONE. `196d19e79` moves no board and I now know WHY — the Icon master's only two `seq` uses are `every write(seq(1) \ 3)`, seq as the DRIVEN expression, which always worked. Had I trusted it I would have shipped a witness that proves nothing, which is the exact false-green our own gates carry a detector-proof arm to prevent. **THE GAP IS NAMED, NOT LEFT QUIET:** the family is declared in `config/MODES.tsv` as `m3,m4` (measured in both modes, not guessed from the family name, as that file's own warning requires) so it is ONE COMMAND from absorption — but I did not run that command, because `util_build_master_suite.py --additive` RENUMBERS THE WHOLE MASTER (463 insertions / 443 deletions on ALL.csv) and absorbs the other 20 undeclared families with it, including other seats' pending witnesses and `icon_display_builtin_unimplemented`. That is a denominator change belonging to whoever owns the master, not a side effect of adding one witness. ⛔ ALSO LEARNED THE HARD WAY: `--additive` WRITES WITHOUT `--write` — there is no dry run on that path, and I reverted the master twice before I understood that.
⭐ **A SECOND DEFECT, FOUND WHILE MINTING THE FIRST AND DELIBERATELY NOT PINNED: THE LIMITATION OPERATOR OVER A CONJUNCTION YIELDS EXACTLY ONE RESULT.** `((1|2|3) & 7) \ 5` gives SCRIP one 7 against Arizona's three; `((1 to 3) & 7) \ 5` the same; `(seq(3,2) & 7) \ 4` one against four. ⭐ THE CONTROL ARM IS THE POINT: `(1|2|3) \ 5` gives `1 2 3` in BOTH, so `\` alone is correct and it is losing resumption specifically over a CONJUNCTION — one defect in the limitation lowering, not a per-generator gap. - **CFO-24 (2026-09-08 20:0x-20:5x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET) — THE RUNTIME DEFINE CLASS IS FOUR FAILURES NOT TWO, MODE 3 NEVER RUNS THE DEFINE BOX, AND THE OBVIOUS CURE REGRESSES THREE MASTER ENTRIES — ABLATED AND BACKED OUT, TREE LEFT CLEAN.** The ceo routed hq_P's class to me (two defects, one mechanism, grade on BOTH witnesses). Both hq_P witnesses reproduce verbatim. ⭐ THERE IS A THIRD SYMPTOM NOBODY NAMED: wA in MODE 4 prints `orig(x)`/`orig(x)` — the runtime redefinition performed inside a function is **silently ignored**, no crash, no diagnostic. So the class is FOUR cells (wB m3 new/new, wB m4 new/new, wA m3 orig+SIGSEGV, wA m4 orig/orig), and a seat grading only m3 fixes the crash and leaves m4 wrong with nothing saying so. THE MECHANISM, top to bottom: the crash is a jump to address 0 because `rt_sno_runtime_define` (rt.c:609) sets `p->fn = 0` and zeroes the `alpha$<name>` cell and never installs the new entry — its caller `_DEFINE_` has the entry label in hand and does not pass it; the static half bakes `body_cell$F: .quad LBL__F2` and BOTH define sites emit `lea rax,[rip+LBL__F2]`, so the first statement seals the second's body; and the IR ALREADY KNOWS BETTER — each bind node carries its own `LIT_NAME` entry and there is even an accessor, `ir_define_bind_entry()` at IR.h:197 — the dedup is in `scrip.c`'s dentry construction, which resolves the entry by looking up the FUNCTION NAME in `s2->proc_table`, one row per name. Also `bb_define.cpp:424` is a two-medium disagreement on one line: text uses the statement's label, binary uses `rt_define_query`'s per-name answer. ⭐⭐ THE FACT THAT DECIDES THE CURE, and I proved it rather than inferred it: I wrote a candidate runtime binder with a diagnostic gated on `SCRIP_DEFINE_BIND_DIAG=1`, called from the DEFINE site in role 6 which `bb_define()` dispatches for BOTH mediums. In m4 it fires twice with the right per-statement entries AND CURES wB. **In m3 it never fires at all** — the emitted call is in the asm (grep counts 2) and mode 3 never runs it. Mode 3 binds DEFINE entirely at slab-seal time, so ANY cure written only in the template moves m4 and leaves m3 byte-for-byte unchanged. ⛔ AND THE OBVIOUS CURE IS WRONG, MEASURED NOT GUESSED: making dentry use the bind node's own entry cures wB m4 and XPASSes `user_function_replace_7`, but REGRESSES `user_function_26`, `user_function_replace_10` and `user_function_replace_branch_1` in m4 — caught by the SNOBOL4 master as a control arm, against hq_U's run 29 s earlier on clean `e4ff7e9c7` which had zero unmarked m4 non-PASS. Cause: a DEFINE with NO explicit second argument must bind to the `<fname>_α` ACTIVATION, and resolving its own body label routes around it. The two arms are COUPLED — restricting the patch to explicit retargets removes the regression AND removes the cure, because wB's first DEFINE has an implicit entry. NOT CURED; candidate BACKED OUT and the tree left clean at `397b3bd22` so no seat measures a board against a half-cure. Full write-up, four-cell table, backtrace and the ablation: `FINDING-2026-09-08-cfo-the-runtime-define-class-is-four-failures-mode-3-never-runs-the-define-box-and-the-obvious-cure-regresses-three-master-entries.md`. ⭐ THE CEO'S 20:4x QUESTION IS ANSWERED AND HIS HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the three m4 reds on my dirty tree were NOT the uncommitted Icon lowerer edit — that had already landed as `196d19e79` before the board ran — they were this DEFINE candidate in `src/driver/scrip.c`. - **CFO-23 (2026-09-08 19:5x-20:2x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET -> NONET) — A REAL ICON SEMANTIC CURED THAT MOVES NO BOARD, THE PROGRAM IT WAS CHASING RE-DIAGNOSED, AND BOTH ICON ROWS RELEASED ON THE SNOBOL4 TELEGRAM (SCRIP `196d19e79`).** THE CURE: `seq()` is one of Icon's FIVE function generators (bal, find, key, seq, upto) and `icn_call_allow_gen()` in `src/lower/lower_icon.c:68` listed only three, so `is_resumable()` said no and the resume GOTO of an enclosing `suspend` was wired to the FAIL node instead of to the TO box. `suspend seq(3,2) & 7` yielded ONE 7 against Arizona's unbounded stream; `suspend seq(3,2)` alone was always right, which is why it hid. The IR is the whole proof in two lines — for `(1 to 9) & 7` the resume goto reads `8@ 2 2 GOTO` (node 2 is the TO box), for `seq(1) & 7` it read `8@ 7@ 7@ GOTO` (node 7@ is FAIL) — and `seq` and `to` lower to the SAME `IR_TO` box, so only the predicate ever differed. The IPL's `prime()` is built from `suspend 2 | ((i := seq(3,2)) & (not(...)) & i)`, so `pfactors`/`isprime`/`prdecomp`/`prime` all stopped after their second value. ⛔ I MEASURED BOTH DIRECTIONS WITH THE TWO BINARIES KEPT SIDE BY SIDE rather than trusting the after-number: Arizona 72/90, Jcon 56/82, Icon master 704/704 — IDENTICAL before and after. A correct oracle-checked cure with NO FLIP, and I am recording it as exactly that. `make test` green, 75 arms, exit 0. ⛔ `bal` DELIBERATELY LEFT OUT: its own generation is short (`every bal('+',,,"a+b+c")` gives 2 where Arizona gives `2 4`), and marking a short generator resumable turns a wrong FINITE answer into an INFINITE resumption — `bal(...) & 7` then yields 7 forever, a HANG where there was a FAIL. I added it, measured that, and backed it out. Cure bal's generation first, list it second. ⭐ I AM CORRECTING MY OWN 09-07 CENSUS LINE: `ilib` is NOT "a crash, and the crash is the finding" in the Icon library — it dies in the SHARED CALL-VALUE SPINE. `rt_call_value_resume_h` (`src/runtime/by_name_dispatch.c:1069`) dereferences `*hslot` as an `ICN_OPGEN_t *` and reads `g->magic` after testing only NON-NULL; when an `IR_CALL_VALUE` beta is entered in an activation whose ALPHA never ran, the cell holds the previous activation's handle instead of the 0 that alpha writes. Eight-line oracle-clean witness (Arizona prints A / 463410420 / B, rc=0), gdb backtrace and a full ablation — neither half alone crashes; the trigger is apply of a procedure value held in a PARAMETER, callee leaving live choice points, resumed THROUGH A LIMITATION — are in `FINDING-2026-09-08-cfo-a-call-value-resume-site-reads-a-handle-cell-its-own-alpha-never-wrote-and-segfaults-on-the-garbage.md`. ⛔ THE CRASH IS PRE-EXISTING AND I PROVED IT RATHER THAN ASSERTING IT: the baseline binary crashes on the same witness, so the `seq` cure did not cause it. But it DOES change which garbage sits in the cell — `ilib` HANGS at 125 lines without the cure and CRASHES at line 9 with it — which means **ilib's CRASH-vs-HANG verdict is not a measurement of anything until the spine is cured**, and no seat should report movement either way. ⭐ AND THE ROW'S OWN DONE-WHEN LIES: it copies only `<p>.icn`/`<p>.dat` into its scratch dir, but `io.icn` shells `ls io.[ids][tca][dnt]` and `ls io.i?n io.d?t io.s?d`, so `io` reads RED (`< io.std`) on a program that is CURED — measured in a directory containing `io.std`, io matches with ZERO differing lines. Every runaway this seat has logged came from a `t.bin` or a `scrip --run` under `/tmp/csh_run_*` or a seat scratchpad; the population that can produce one has gone from four seats to eight while the box stayed at 16 cores; and the seven runaways of CFO-1 held half this box for three days and were ended by a REBOOT rather than by anyone's decision, because no grant existed. Same bound as proposed 2026-09-07: any such process older than 2 hours.
- **CFO-21 (2026-09-08 19:2x-19:4x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET) — io GOES 73 -> 7 DIFFERING LINES ON TWO CURES (SCRIP `809f18f9f`, `e2bb6141e`).** `rename()` had an arity and no body; then the real one: **a file argument inside `write()`/`writes()` must redirect the arguments after it**, and SCRIP only ever honoured a file in position 0, so every later file argument was written as a VALUE to the destination chosen at the start -- io.icn's four such calls came out interleaved on stdout with the files empty. ⛔ I GOT THE NEWLINE RULE WRONG ONCE AND THE MEASUREMENT CORRECTED ME: my first version emitted the newline only when the destination being left had been WRITTEN to, which is the intuitive rule. Arizona emits one on EVERY file argument except one in position 0. The case that settles it is a REPEATED file argument -- `write(">stdout", t1, ">1c", t2, t2, ">2c", &output)` puts an EMPTY LINE in t2 for the second t2, which a was-it-written rule cannot produce -- and `write(f,"S",g,"T")` proves the position-0 exemption. SCRIP now reproduces the four-call witness byte-for-byte. ⛔ AND A WRONG COUNT OF MY OWN, caught: io runs `ls io.s?d` through `system()`, so measuring it in a scratch dir WITHOUT io.std produced two diff lines that were my harness's fault, not SCRIP's. Re-measured in a faithful directory. io's remaining 7 are two further isolated defects, neither touched: sort() orders files by descriptor where Arizona orders by image, and read() does not treat `\r` as a line terminator where Arizona does. Icon master 704/704, Arizona 70/90, Jcon 55/82 -- all unchanged, io still red until its last 7 go.
- **CFO-22 (2026-09-08 19:2x-20:0x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET) — THE ARIZONA io TEST IS CURED, 73 DIFFERING LINES TO ZERO IN BOTH MODES (SCRIP `809f18f9f`, `e2bb6141e`, `384033ade`). Arizona 70/90 -> **71/90 BOTH MODES**, row written by the runner at `384033ade`.** Five defects, each isolated against Arizona icont BEFORE it was written: `rename()` had an arity and no body · a file argument inside `write()`/`writes()` only ever worked in position 0 · the u/t open mode was PARSED AND THROWN AWAY · `sort()` left files in insertion order · stdout was not flushed before `pclose`. ⭐ THE ONE I WANT REMEMBERED: **the u/t mode was computed and discarded.** `icn_open_cmode` already worked out `un` for u/t and used it only to append `'b'` to the C mode -- a no-op on Linux -- and dropped it ENTIRELY for a pipe, which is the case the test exercises. So read() had ONE behaviour regardless of what the program asked for. A flag that is parsed, not used, and not refused is worse than an unimplemented one: the program says `"up"`, nothing complains, and the answer is silently the other mode's. ⛔ AND THE ONLY m3/m4 DIVERGENCE IN THE TEST, named because that is the shape the one-engine rule exists to prevent: mode 4 alone printed `--- closing output pipe` AFTER the pipe's own output, because our stdout was still buffered when `pclose` let the child through. m3 passed and m4 failed on the SAME SOURCE. Cured by flushing stdout before pclose, as iconx does. ⛔ TWO MISTAKES OF MINE ON THE WAY, both caught by building: I got the write() newline rule wrong first (was-it-written instead of positional -- a REPEATED file argument settles it), and I added a house-style separator line WITHOUT its closing `*/`, which commented out `icn_open_cmode` and produced an undefined-reference at link. The separators are 200 chars wide and I had only ever seen them truncated at 150. Icon master 704/704 both modes and Jcon 55/82 both modes, unchanged throughout.

- ⭐ **THE SAME RUNNER TWICE, SAME TREE, TWO LOADS (cfo 2026-09-13):** `test_snobol4_ladder.sh --only 22` (13 witnesses × 2 modes) · **3.6 s at load 6** and **31 s at load 29** = **8.6x**; `test_snobol4_ladder.sh --to 22` (166 witnesses × 2 modes) · **1m45s at load 6**, **4m20s at load 30**, **1m40s at load 2.9** = **2.5x**. ⛔ THE LOAD PENALTY IS PROPORTIONALLY WORSE ON THE SHORT RUNNER — which is the one a seat re-runs dozens of times inside one cure cycle. A short board's quoted cost is the least trustworthy number in this table.
- `test_icon_ladder.sh` (344 witnesses × 2 modes, rungs 0..42) · **51 s** · load ~3 · SCRIP `fbb1db6ef` · `test_snocone_ladder.sh` (123 × 2) · ~20 s · `test_prolog_ladder.sh` (284 × 2) · ~2 min, all on the same tree and used as the shared-node control arms for a runtime call-path cure.
- incremental `make` **after a pull that brought a Makefile change** · **24 s** · load ~6 — and the freshness guard refuses rc=2 on the very next runner until you pay it, so a pull is not free even when it touches no source you care about. A runtime-`.c`-only edit · **2.6 s** · load ~3. A 9-commit pull touching driver + templates · **2m19s** · load 6.
- `make preflight` · **5.1 s** · **42 arms** (it was 33 when this digest's CLAUDE.md was written and 35 four sittings ago — the cheap target is growing) · load ~3 · SCRIP `fbb1db6ef`.
- `util_add_ladder_witness.py` per witness (oracle cut twice + stdin probe + full master re-serialisation) · **~1 s** · load ~6 — thirteen witnesses minted in one pass; the cost of the sanctioned path is not the reason anyone hand-edits a master.
- ⛔ THE FAIL-ONCE PROOF IS NOT FREE AND IS WORTH ITS PRICE: thirteen corrupt-restore-regrade cycles through `--only 22` · **~68 s total** at load 6 (13 × ~5 s) — that is the entire cost of proving every new witness can actually fail.

## THE CFO LOOP
1. `check` the inbox; route economy asks to Lon in chat, grants into this file the same turn.
2. The ledger line (above), every sitting and on every Lon "next".
3. The runaway census; kill only under a recorded grant.
4. `next` — the EASIEST FREE rank-0 row; cure; measure through the suite's own runner; flip to `coo`; repeat.
5. A landing = pushed + the runner's row rewritten + the ledger line; the banner verdict is computed, never typed.
